local function espRoutine(localValue93)
    if not localValue93 or type(localValue93) ~= "string" then return nil end

    localValue93 = localValue93:match("^%s*(.-)%s*$")

    local localValue94 = localValue91[localValue93]
    if localValue94 then return localValue94 end

    for name, id in pairs(localValue91) do
        if name.lower(name) == localValue93.lower(localValue93) then
            return id
        end
    end
    return nil
end

end

            for _, localValue124 in ipairs(State.mayersConnections) do pcall(function() localValue124.Disconnect(localValue124) end) end
            State.mayersConnections = {}
            State.localValue82 = {}
            if State.mayersGuiConnection then pcall(function() State.mayersGuiConnection.Disconnect(State.mayersGuiConnection) end) State.mayersGuiConnection = nil end
            if State.mayersDescendantConnection then pcall(function() State.mayersDescendantConnection.Disconnect(State.mayersDescendantConnection) end) State.mayersDescendantConnection = nil end
        end
end

local function disableInfiniteLunge()
    if State.infiniteLungeCharacterConnection then
        State.infiniteLungeCharacterConnection.Disconnect(State.infiniteLungeCharacterConnection)
        State.infiniteLungeCharacterConnection = nil
    end

    if State.originalLungeBoost ~= nil then
        local char = LocalPlayer.Character
        if char then
            pcall(function() char:SetAttribute("lungeboost", State.originalLungeBoost) end)
        end
        State.originalLungeBoost = nil
    end

    if State.disabledLungeConnections then
        for localValue125 = 1, #State.disabledLungeConnections do
            local localValue124 = State.disabledLungeConnections[localValue125]
            if localValue124 then
                pcall(function() localValue124.Enable(localValue124) end)
            end
        end
        State.disabledLungeConnections = nil
    end
end

local function enableInfiniteLunge()
    if getPlayerRole(LocalPlayer) ~= "killer" then return end
    if State.infiniteLungeCharacterConnection then return end

    local char = LocalPlayer.Character

    if char then
        pcall(function()
            State.originalLungeBoost = char:GetAttribute("lungeboost")
            char:SetAttribute("lungeboost", 999999)
        end)
    end

    pcall(function()
        local child9 = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Remotes")
        local child10 = child9 and child9:FindFirstChild("Attacks")
        local instance2 = child10 and child10:FindFirstChild("LungeDetect")
        if instance2 and instance2:IsA("RemoteEvent") then
            local localValue126 = getconnections(instance2.OnClientEvent)
            local localValue127 = {}
            for localValue125 = 1, #localValue126 do
                local localValue124 = localValue126[localValue125]
                pcall(function()
                    localValue124.Disable(localValue124)
                    localValue127[#localValue127 + 1] = localValue124
                end)
            end
            State.disabledLungeConnections = localValue127
        end
    end)

    State.infiniteLungeCharacterConnection = LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded.Connect(LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded,function(newChar)
        if not Config.infiniteLungeEnabled then return end
        pcall(function()
            State.originalLungeBoost = newChar:GetAttribute("lungeboost")
            newChar:SetAttribute("lungeboost", (999999.0))
        end)
    end)
end

local geometryRoutine, Spear_PingSec
do

    local localValue133 = nil
    function Spear_PingSec()
        if localValue133 == nil then
            pcall(function()
                localValue133 = game:GetService("Stats")["Network"]["ServerStatsItem"]["Data Ping"]
            end)
        end

    end
    function geometryRoutine(localValue135, char, posNow)
        local timestamp = tick()

            localValue135.char = char
            localValue135.hist = {}
            localValue135.vector3 = Vector3.new(0, 0, (0.0))
            localValue135.turn = (0.0)
        end
        if localValue135.turn == nil then localValue135.turn = 0 end
        local hist = localValue135.hist
        table.insert(hist, { pos = posNow, t = timestamp })
        while #hist > 1 and hist[(1.0)]["t"] < timestamp - 0.4 do table.remove(hist, 1) end
        if #hist >= 2 then
            local localValue136 = hist[#hist - 1]
            local localValue137 = hist[#hist]
            local localValue138 = localValue137.t - localValue136.t
            if localValue138 > 0.001 then
                local localValue139 = (localValue137.pos - localValue136.pos) / localValue138
                local instance = Vector3.new(localValue139.X, 0, localValue139.Z)
                local rootPart2 = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                if rootPart2 and rootPart2:IsA("BasePart") then
                    local ok, av = pcall(function() return rootPart2.AssemblyLinearVelocity end)
do local localValue140=(86*84+3)%42 end
                    if ok and typeof(av) == "Vector3" then
                        local vector4 = Vector3.new(av.X, 0, av.Z)
                        if vector4.Magnitude > 0.5 and vector4.Magnitude < 100 then
                            instance = instance:Lerp(vector4, 0.5)
                        end
                    end
                end

                if instance.Magnitude > 24 then instance = instance.Unit * 24 end

                local localValue141 = 0
                if localValue135.vector3.Magnitude > 2 and instance.Magnitude > (2.0) then
                    local localValue113 = localValue135.vector3.Dot(localValue135.vector3,instance) / (localValue135.vector3.Magnitude * instance.Magnitude)

        localValue135.vector3 = vector5

    end
end

    function ApplyGhostGateToObj(instance4, enabled)
        if not instance4 or not instance4.Parent then return end
        local localValue263 = instance4:IsA("BasePart") and {instance4} or {}
        if not instance4:IsA("BasePart") then
            local function gateRoutine(localValue119)
                for _, localValue122 in ipairs(localValue119.GetChildren(localValue119)) do
                    if localValue122:IsA("BasePart") then table.insert(localValue263, localValue122) end
                    gateRoutine(localValue122)
                end
            end
            gateRoutine(instance4)
        end
        for _, part in ipairs(localValue263) do
            if part:IsA("BasePart") then
                if enabled then
                    if not State.ghostGateOriginals[part] then
                        State.ghostGateOriginals[part] = { CanCollide = part.CanCollide, Transparency = part.Transparency }
                    end
                    part.CanCollide = false
                    part.Transparency = 0.6
                else
                    if State.ghostGateOriginals[part] then
                        part.CanCollide = State.ghostGateOriginals[part]["CanCollide"]
                        part.Transparency = State.ghostGateOriginals[part]["Transparency"]
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end

    function ApplyAntiLoopWindow()
        if not State.localValue58 then return end

        local child9 = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Remotes")
        if not child9 then return end

            end
        end)
    end

    function DisableAntiLoopWindow()
        local child9 = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Remotes")
        if not child9 then return end
        local child16 = child9:FindFirstChild("Window")
        if not child16 then return end

        local child18 = child16:FindFirstChild("VaultCompleteEvent")
        local child19 = child16:FindFirstChild("VaultCompleteEventpart1")

            for _, localValue278 in ipairs(localValue274) do

                        if child18 then
                            child18:FireServer(localValue278, false)
                        end

                        if child19 then
                            child19.FireServer(child19)
                        end
                    end)
                end)
                task.wait(0.05)
            end
        end)
    end

            end

local function applyHitboxModifier(player, char)
    if not Config.hitboxModifierEnabled then return end
    if not char then return end

    local localValue198 = getPlayerRole(player)
    local localValue286 = (localValue198 == "killer") and Config.killerHitboxScale or Config.survivorHitboxScale
    local localValue287 = localValue286 / (100.0)

    local rootPart2 = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if rootPart2 and rootPart2:IsA("BasePart") then
        if not State.hitboxOriginalSizes[rootPart2] then
            State.hitboxOriginalSizes[rootPart2] = rootPart2.Size
        end

        local localValue288 = 2
        local vector8 = Vector3.new(localValue288 * localValue287, localValue288 * localValue287, localValue288 * localValue287)

        pcall(function()
            rootPart2.Size = vector8
            rootPart2.Transparency = 1
            rootPart2.CanCollide = false
        end)
    end
end

local function hitboxRoutine(player, char)
    if not char then return end
    local rootPart2 = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if rootPart2 and rootPart2:IsA("BasePart") then
        local localValue289 = State.hitboxOriginalSizes[rootPart2]
        if localValue289 then
            pcall(function()
                rootPart2.Size = localValue289
                rootPart2.Transparency = 1
            end)
            State.hitboxOriginalSizes[rootPart2] = nil
        end
    end
end

local function hitboxRoutine2()
    if not Config.hitboxModifierEnabled then return end
    for _, player in ipairs(Players.GetPlayers(Players)) do
        if player ~= LocalPlayer and player.Character then applyHitboxModifier(player, player.Character) end
    end
end

local function hitboxRoutine3()
    for _, player in ipairs(Players.GetPlayers(Players)) do
        if player ~= LocalPlayer and player.Character then hitboxRoutine(player, player.Character) end
    end
end

local function applyHitboxESP(player, char)
    if player == LocalPlayer then return end
    local rootPart2 = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not rootPart2 then return end
    local localValue290 = State.hitboxEspObjects[player]

    if localValue290 and localValue290.Parent then
        if localValue290.Adornee == rootPart2 then return end
        localValue290.Destroy(localValue290)
    end

    local localValue198 = getPlayerRole(player)
    local color = (localValue198 == "killer") and Config.killerHitboxColor or Config.survivorHitboxColor
    local localValue291 = Instance.new("SelectionBox")
    localValue291.Adornee = rootPart2; localValue291.Color3 = color; localValue291.LineThickness = 0.10
    localValue291.SurfaceTransparency = Config.hitboxEspOutlineOnly and (1.0) or Config.hitboxFillTransparency
    localValue291.SurfaceColor3 = color; localValue291.Parent = espRoutine2()
    State.hitboxEspObjects[player] = localValue291
end

local function hitboxRoutine4(player)
    local localValue291 = State.hitboxEspObjects[player]
    if localValue291 then pcall(function() localValue291.Destroy(localValue291) end); State.hitboxEspObjects[player] = nil end
end

local function hitboxRoutine5()
    for player, localValue291 in pairs(State.hitboxEspObjects) do
        if localValue291 and localValue291.Parent then
            local localValue198 = getPlayerRole(player)
            local color = (localValue198 == "killer") and Config.killerHitboxColor or Config.survivorHitboxColor
            localValue291.Color3 = color; localValue291.SurfaceColor3 = color
            localValue291.SurfaceTransparency = Config.hitboxEspOutlineOnly and 1 or Config.hitboxFillTransparency
        end
    end
end

local function enableHitboxESP()
    for _, player in ipairs(Players.GetPlayers(Players)) do
        if player ~= LocalPlayer and player.Character then applyHitboxESP(player, player.Character) end
    end
end

local function disableHitboxESP()
    for player, _ in pairs(State.hitboxEspObjects) do hitboxRoutine4(player) end
end

local hookRoutine12, _ApplySpeed, _StartAntiFallSlow, _StopAntiFallSlow, _StartNoSlowdown, _StopNoSlowdown, SetupMovementForChar
do

    function hookRoutine12()
        if State.SpeedBoost then
            return localValue95 * ((1.0) + math.clamp(tonumber(State.localValue2) or 0.3, (0.0), 2))
        end
        return localValue95
    end

    function _ApplySpeed(localValue112, localValue305)
        if not localValue112 or not localValue112.Parent then return end
        if math.abs(localValue112.WalkSpeed - localValue305) > 0.05 then
            pcall(function() localValue112.WalkSpeed = localValue305 end)
        end
    end

    local function hookRoutine13()
        task.spawn(function()
            local ok, remote = pcall(function()
                return game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):WaitForChild("Remotes", (10.0)):WaitForChild("Mechanics", (10.0)):WaitForChild("Fall", (10.0))
            end)

            local localValue130
            localValue130 = hookmetamethod(game, "__namecall", function(self, ...)
                local localValue306 = getnamecallmethod()
                if State.localValue4 and localValue306 == "FireServer" and rawequal(self, remote) then return end
                return localValue130(self, ...)
            end)
        end)
    end

    function _StartAntiFallSlow(localValue112)
        if State.movConns.antiFall then State.movConns.antiFall["Disconnect"](State.movConns.antiFall) end
        hookRoutine13()
        if not localValue112 then return end
        pcall(function() localValue112:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.FallingDown, false) end)
        State.movConns.antiFall = localValue112.StateChanged.Connect(localValue112.StateChanged,function(_, localValue307)
            if not State.localValue4 then return end
            if localValue307 == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Landed or localValue307 == Enum.HumanoidStateType.GettingUp or localValue307 == Enum.HumanoidStateType.FallingDown then
                pcall(function() localValue112:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Running) end)
                _ApplySpeed(localValue112, hookRoutine12())
            end
        end)
    end

    function _StopAntiFallSlow(localValue112)
        if State.movConns.antiFall then State.movConns.antiFall["Disconnect"](State.movConns.antiFall); State.movConns.antiFall = nil end
        if localValue112 and localValue112.Parent then pcall(function() localValue112:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.FallingDown, true) end) end
    end

    function _StartNoSlowdown(localValue112)
        if State.movConns.noSlow then State.movConns.noSlow["Disconnect"](State.movConns.noSlow); State.movConns.noSlow = nil end
        if not localValue112 then return end
        if getPlayerRole(LocalPlayer) == "killer" then return end
        State.movConns.noSlow = localValue112:GetPropertyChangedSignal("WalkSpeed"):Connect(function()
            if not State.localValue then return end
            if getPlayerRole(LocalPlayer) == "killer" then return end

            local char = LocalPlayer.Character

            if State.safeModeSpeed and char and (char:GetAttribute("IsHooked") or char:GetAttribute("IsCarried") or localValue112.Health <= (50.0)) then
                return
            end

            if State.localValue5 and (hookRoutine4() or IsPlayerDoingAction()) then
                return
            end

            local localValue305 = hookRoutine12()
            if localValue112.WalkSpeed < localValue305 - 0.05 then pcall(function() localValue112.WalkSpeed = localValue305 end) end
        end)
    end

    function _StopNoSlowdown()
        if State.movConns.noSlow then State.movConns.noSlow["Disconnect"](State.movConns.noSlow); State.movConns.noSlow = nil end
    end

    function SetupMovementForChar(char)
        if not char then return end
        local localValue112 = char:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 5)
        if not localValue112 then return end
        if State.localValue4 then _StartAntiFallSlow(localValue112) end
        if State.localValue and getPlayerRole(LocalPlayer) ~= "killer" then _StartNoSlowdown(localValue112) end
        if State.SpeedBoost then
            local localValue305 = hookRoutine12()
            task.wait(0.15)
do local localValue308=359 end
            if not (State.safeModeSpeed and (hookRoutine4() or IsPlayerDoingAction())) then
                _ApplySpeed(localValue112, localValue305)
            end
        end
    end

    LocalPlayer:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Team"):Connect(function()
        hookRoutine2(LocalPlayer)
        if getPlayerRole(LocalPlayer) == "killer" and State.movConns.noSlow then
            State.movConns.noSlow["Disconnect"](State.movConns.noSlow); State.movConns.noSlow = nil
        end
        if getPlayerRole(LocalPlayer) ~= "killer" then
            disableInfiniteLunge()
        end
    end)
end

end

local instanceRoutine5

function IsGeneratorPoint(localValue240)
    if not localValue240 then return false end
    local localValue347 = localValue240:GetAttribute("OriginalName")
    if localValue347 and string.find(tostring(localValue347):lower(), "generatorpoint") then return true end
    if string.find(localValue240.Name, "^GeneratorPoint%d+$") then return true end
    return false
end

function CollectGeneratorPoints(localValue332)
    local localValue348 = {}
    if not localValue332 then return localValue348 end
    local localValue349 = {}
    local function generatorRoutine7(localValue240)
        if localValue349[localValue240] then return end
        if IsGeneratorPoint(localValue240) then
            localValue349[localValue240] = true
            table.insert(localValue348, localValue240)
        end
    end
    local function instanceRoutine8(localValue119)
        for _, localValue122 in ipairs(localValue119.GetChildren(localValue119)) do
            generatorRoutine7(localValue122)
            instanceRoutine8(localValue122)
        end
    end
    instanceRoutine8(localValue332)
    table.sort(localValue348, function(localValue136, localValue137)
        local name2 = localValue136:GetAttribute("OriginalName") or localValue136.Name
        local name3 = localValue137:GetAttribute("OriginalName") or localValue137.Name
        return tostring(name2) < tostring(name3)
    end)
    return localValue348
end

function FindGenForPoint(localValue281)
    if not localValue281 then return nil end
    local localValue213 = localValue281
    for _ = 1, 6 do
        local localValue119 = localValue213.Parent
        if not localValue119 then break end
        if localValue119:IsA("Model") or localValue119:IsA("Folder") then
            local name = localValue119.Name.lower(localValue119.Name)
            if string.find(name, "generator") or string.find(name, "gens") then
                return localValue119
            end
            for _, localValue122 in ipairs(localValue119.GetChildren(localValue119)) do
                if IsGeneratorPoint(localValue122) then return localValue119 end
            end
        end
        localValue213 = localValue119
    end
    for _, localValue332 in ipairs(State.mapObjects.Generators) do
        if localValue332 and localValue332.Parent and localValue281:IsDescendantOf(localValue332) then return localValue332 end
    end
    return nil
end

function GetAllGenerators()
    local localValue350 = {}
    local localValue349 = {}
    local function generatorRoutine8(localValue332)
        if localValue332 and localValue332.Parent and not localValue349[localValue332] then
            localValue349[localValue332] = true
            table.insert(localValue350, localValue332)
        end
    end
    for _, localValue332 in ipairs(State.mapObjects.Generators) do generatorRoutine8(localValue332) end
    local localValue266 = workspace:FindFirstChild("Map")
    if localValue266 then
        for _, localValue351 in ipairs({"Generators", "Generator", "Gens"}) do
            local child31 = localValue266:FindFirstChild(localValue351)
            if child31 then
                for _, localValue332 in ipairs(child31.GetChildren(child31)) do generatorRoutine8(localValue332) end
            end
        end
    end
    return localValue350
end

function GetPointPosition(localValue281)
    if localValue281:IsA("BasePart") then return localValue281.Position end
    local part = localValue281:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart")
    if part then return part.Position end
    return nil
end

function PerformGenBoost()
    task.spawn(function()
        local ok, err = pcall(function()
            local char = LocalPlayer.Character
            if not char then
                notify("GenBoost", "Character not found!", 2)
                return
            end
            local rootPart = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            if not rootPart then
                notify("GenBoost", "Character HumanoidRootPart not found!", 2)
                return
            end

            local child32 = char:FindFirstChild("CheckInterractable")
            local localValue352 = child32 and child32:GetAttribute("isRepairing") or false
            if not localValue352 then
                notify("GenBoost", "Repair a generator first!", (2.0))
                return
            end

            local localValue350 = GetAllGenerators()
            local localValue353 = nil
            local localValue354 = math.huge
            for _, localValue332 in ipairs(localValue350) do
                for _, localValue281 in ipairs(CollectGeneratorPoints(localValue332)) do
                    local pos = GetPointPosition(localValue281)
                    local dist = pos and (pos - rootPart.Position)["Magnitude"] or math.huge
                    if dist < localValue354 then
                        localValue354 = dist
                        localValue353 = localValue281
                    end
                end
            end

            local localValue332 = localValue353 and FindGenForPoint(localValue353)
            if not localValue332 then
                local localValue355 = nil
                local localValue356 = math.huge
                for _, localValue357 in ipairs(localValue350) do
                    for _, localValue281 in ipairs(CollectGeneratorPoints(localValue357)) do
                        local pos = GetPointPosition(localValue281)
                        local dist = pos and (pos - rootPart.Position)["Magnitude"] or math.huge
                        if dist < localValue356 then
                            localValue356 = dist
                            localValue355 = localValue281
                        end
                    end
                end
                if localValue355 then
                    localValue353 = localValue355
                    localValue332 = FindGenForPoint(localValue355)
                end
            end

            if not localValue332 then
                notify("GenBoost", "Could not find current generator!", (2.0))
                return
            end

            local localValue348 = CollectGeneratorPoints(localValue332)
            if #localValue348 == 0 then
                notify("GenBoost", "No generator points resolved!", 2)
                return
            end

            local child33 = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Remotes")
                and ReplicatedStorage.Remotes.FindFirstChild(ReplicatedStorage.Remotes,"Generator")
                and ReplicatedStorage.Remotes.Generator["FindFirstChild"](ReplicatedStorage.Remotes.Generator,"RepairEvent")
            if not child33 then child33 = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("RepairEvent", true) end
            if not child33 then
                notify("GenBoost", "RepairEvent remote not found!", 2)
                return
            end

            local localValue358 = localValue353
            local localValue116 = false
            for _, player in ipairs(localValue348) do
                if player == localValue358 then localValue116 = true break end
            end
            if not localValue358 or not localValue116 then localValue358 = localValue348[1] end

            for _, player in ipairs(localValue348) do
                if player ~= localValue358 then
                    child33:FireServer(player, true)
                    task.wait(0.4)
                    child33:FireServer(localValue358, true)
                    task.wait(0.15)
                    child33:FireServer(localValue358, false)
                    task.wait(0.15)
                end
            end
            for _, player in ipairs(localValue348) do
                if player ~= localValue358 then
                    child33:FireServer(player, true)
                    task.wait(0.3)
                end
            end
            child33:FireServer(localValue358, false)
            task.wait(0.2)
            local localValue359 = GetPointPosition(localValue358)
            if localValue359 and rootPart then
                pcall(function() rootPart.Anchored = false end)
                pcall(function() rootPart.CFrame = CFrame.new(localValue359 + Vector3.new(0, 1.5, (0.0))) end)
            end
            task.wait(0.3)
            pcall(instanceRoutine5)

            notify("GenBoost", "Successfully applied generator Boost!", 2)
        end)
        if not ok then
            notify("GenBoost", "Critical Error: " .. tostring(err), 2)
        end
    end)
end

function DestroyGenBoostButton()
    if State.genBoostConnection then
        pcall(function() State.genBoostConnection.Disconnect(State.genBoostConnection) end)
        State.genBoostConnection = nil
    end
    if State.genBoostGui then
        pcall(function() State.genBoostGui.Destroy(State.genBoostGui) end)
        State.genBoostGui = nil
    end
end

function BuildGenBoostGui(localValue360)
    if not localValue360 or State.genBoostGui then return end
    local gui = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
    gui.Name = "NekopoiHubGenBoost"
    gui.ResetOnSpawn = false
    gui.Parent = LocalPlayer:WaitForChild("PlayerGui")

    local localValue120 = Instance.new("ImageButton")
    localValue120.Name = "GenBoostBtn"
    localValue120.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    localValue120.BorderSizePixel = 0
    localValue120.AutoButtonColor = false
    localValue120.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(76, 76)
    localValue120.Image = "rbxassetid://129980991442403"
    localValue120.ImageTransparency = 0.5
    localValue120.Draggable = false
    localValue120.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new((1.0), 0.5)
    localValue120.Position = UDim2.new(
        localValue360.Position.X["Scale"],
        localValue360.Position.X["Offset"] + 100,
        localValue360.Position.Y["Scale"],
        localValue360.Position.Y["Offset"]
    )
    localValue120.Parent = gui

    localValue120.MouseButton1Click.Connect(localValue120.MouseButton1Click,PerformGenBoost)
    localValue120.MouseButton1Click.Connect(localValue120.MouseButton1Click,function()
        localValue120.ImageColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 128, 0)
        task.delay(0.3, function()
            localValue120.ImageColor3 = Color3.fromRGB((255.0), 255, 255)
        end)
    end)

    State.genBoostGui = gui
end

function CreateGenBoostButton()
    if State.genBoostGui then return end

    local child34 = LocalPlayer:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui")
    if child34 then
        local child35 = child34:FindFirstChild("Survivor-mob")
        if child35 then
            local child36 = child35:FindFirstChild("Controls")
            local localValue360 = child36 and child36:FindFirstChild("crouch")
            if localValue360 and localValue360:IsA("GuiButton") then
                BuildGenBoostGui(localValue360)
                return
            end
        end
    end

    if State.genBoostConnection then return end
    child34 = child34 or LocalPlayer:WaitForChild("PlayerGui")
    State.genBoostConnection = child34.ChildAdded.Connect(child34.ChildAdded,function(localValue122)
        if localValue122 and localValue122.Name == "Survivor-mob" then
            if State.genBoostConnection then
                State.genBoostConnection.Disconnect(State.genBoostConnection)
                State.genBoostConnection = nil
            end
            task.spawn(function()
                local child36 = localValue122:WaitForChild("Controls", 5)
                local localValue360 = child36 and child36:WaitForChild("crouch", 5)
                if localValue360 and localValue360:IsA("GuiButton") and Config.showGenBoostButton then
                    BuildGenBoostGui(localValue360)
                end
            end)
        end
    end)
end

registerTask("KillerWarn", 0.1, function()
    if not Config.killerWarningEnabled then
        local character2 = LocalPlayer.Character
        local rootPart3 = character2 and character2:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if rootPart3 then
            local child37 = rootPart3:FindFirstChild("KillerWarn")
            if child37 then child37.Destroy(child37) end
        end
        return
    end
    local character2 = LocalPlayer.Character
    local rootPart3 = character2 and character2:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not rootPart3 then return end
    local localValue361 = math.huge
    for player, localValue222 in pairs(State.espObjects) do
        if player and player.Parent then
            local localValue198 = getPlayerRole(player)
            if localValue198 == "killer" then

local instanceRoutine12

    local function hookRoutine16()

        local char = LocalPlayer.Character
        if not char then return end

            return
        end

            return
        end

        local localValue305 = hookRoutine12()
        if localValue305 <= 0 then return end
        local localValue390 = math.clamp(tonumber(State.localValue2) or 0.3, (0.0), 2)
        local localValue391 = localValue95 * localValue390
        State._jitterFlip = not State._jitterFlip
        local target = State._jitterFlip and (localValue305 + localValue391) or localValue305
        if math.abs(localValue112.WalkSpeed - target) > 0.1 then
            pcall(function() localValue112.WalkSpeed = target end)
        end
    end

    local function getAimLockPoint(char)
        if not char then return nil end

        if Config.aimLockPart == "Head" then
            local partOrPosition = char:FindFirstChild("Head")
            if partOrPosition then return partOrPosition.Position end
        end
        local partOrPosition3 = char:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso")
        if partOrPosition3 then return partOrPosition3.Position end
        local partOrPosition4 = char:FindFirstChild("Torso")
        if partOrPosition4 then return partOrPosition4.Position end
        local rootPart2 = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if rootPart2 then
            return rootPart2.Position + (Config.aimLockPart == "Head" and Vector3.new((0.0), 2.5, 0) or Vector3.new(0, 1.2, 0))
        end
        return nil
    end

    local function isValidAimTarget(player, char, localValue112)
        if not char or not localValue112 or localValue112.Health <= 0 then return false end

        if localValue112.Health < (50.0) then return false end

        if char:GetAttribute("IsHooked") then return false end
        if char:GetAttribute("IsCarried") then return false end
        if player:GetAttribute("IsHooked") then return false end
        if player:GetAttribute("IsCarried") then return false end

        local localValue132 = localValue112.GetState(localValue112)
        if localValue132 == Enum.HumanoidStateType.PlatformStanding then return false end
        if localValue132 == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Physics and localValue112.PlatformStand then return false end

        local rootPart2 = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if rootPart2 and rootPart2.Anchored then return false end

        return true
    end

    local function aimRoutine(currentTarget)
        local character = LocalPlayer.Character
        local rootPart3 = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if not rootPart3 then return nil end
        local localValue373 = rootPart3.Position
        local localValue401 = 0.1
        local localValue402 = nil
        local localValue354 = Config.aimLockMaxDistance

        if Config.aimLockTargetType == "Zombie" then
            if currentTarget and typeof(currentTarget) == "Instance" and not currentTarget:IsA("Player") then
                local rootPart2 = GetScpModelRoot(currentTarget)
                if rootPart2 and rootPart2:IsA("BasePart") and currentTarget.Parent then
                    local dist = (rootPart2.Position - localValue373)["Magnitude"]
                    if dist <= Config.aimLockMaxDistance then
                        localValue402 = currentTarget
                        localValue354 = dist - localValue401
                    end
                end
            end
            if tick() - zombieCacheTime > 0.5 then RefreshZombieCache() end
            for localValue125 = 1, #localValue381 do
                local localValue383 = localValue381[localValue125]
                if localValue383 and localValue383.Parent then
                    local rootPart2 = GetScpModelRoot(localValue383)
                    if rootPart2 and rootPart2:IsA("BasePart") then
                        local dist = (rootPart2.Position - localValue373)["Magnitude"]
                        if dist < localValue354 then localValue354 = dist; localValue402 = localValue383 end
                    end
                end
            end
            return localValue402
        end

        if currentTarget and currentTarget.Character then
            local localValue403 = currentTarget.Character
            local humanoid = localValue403:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            local localValue404 = getAimLockPoint(localValue403)
            local localValue405 = true
            if Config.aimLockTargetType == "Killer" and getPlayerRole(currentTarget) ~= "killer" then
                localValue405 = false
            elseif Config.aimLockTargetType == "Survivor" and getPlayerRole(currentTarget) ~= "survivor" then
                localValue405 = false
            end

            if localValue405 and isValidAimTarget(currentTarget, localValue403, humanoid) and localValue404 then
                local localValue406 = (localValue404 - localValue373)["Magnitude"]
                if localValue406 <= Config.aimLockMaxDistance then
                    localValue402 = currentTarget
                    localValue354 = localValue406 - localValue401
                end
            end
        end

        for _, player in ipairs(Players.GetPlayers(Players)) do
            if player == LocalPlayer then continue end
            local char = player.Character
            if not char then continue end
            local localValue112 = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")

            if not isValidAimTarget(player, char, localValue112) then continue end

            local localValue407 = getAimLockPoint(char)
            if not localValue407 then continue end
            local localValue198 = getPlayerRole(player)
            if Config.aimLockTargetType == "Killer" and localValue198 ~= "killer" then continue end
            if Config.aimLockTargetType == "Survivor" and localValue198 ~= "survivor" then continue end
            local dist = (localValue407 - localValue373)["Magnitude"]
            if dist < localValue354 then localValue354 = dist; localValue402 = player end
        end
        return localValue402
    end

    function startCameraLock()
        if State.aimLockRenderConnection then State.aimLockRenderConnection.Disconnect(State.aimLockRenderConnection) end
        State.aimLockTarget = nil; State.aimLockTargetTime = (0.0); State.aimLockTargetPart = nil
        State.aimLockRenderConnection = RunService.RenderStepped.Connect(RunService.RenderStepped,function(localValue138)
            if not Config.aimLockEnabled then return end
            if Config.aimLockMode == "Hold to Lock" and not State.aimLockHeld then
                State.aimLockTarget = nil; State.aimLockTargetPart = nil; return
            end
            local character = LocalPlayer.Character
            if not character then return end
            local rootPart3 = character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            if not rootPart3 then return end
            State.aimLockTargetTime = State.aimLockTargetTime + localValue138
            if State.aimLockTargetTime >= 0.15 then
                State.aimLockTargetTime = 0
                State.aimLockTarget = aimRoutine(State.aimLockTarget)
            end
            local target = State.aimLockTarget
            if not target then State.aimLockTargetPart = nil; return end

            local localValue407
            if typeof(target) == "Instance" and not target:IsA("Player") then
                local instance16 = GetScpModelRoot(target)
                if not instance16 or not instance16:IsA("BasePart") or not target.Parent then
                    State.aimLockTarget = nil; State.aimLockTargetPart = nil; return
                end
                localValue407 = instance16.Position
            else
                local localValue408 = target.Character
                if not localValue408 then State.aimLockTarget = nil; State.aimLockTargetPart = nil; return end
                local humanoid2 = localValue408:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")

                if not isValidAimTarget(target, localValue408, humanoid2) then
                    State.aimLockTarget = nil; State.aimLockTargetPart = nil; return
                end

                localValue407 = getAimLockPoint(localValue408)
            end
            if not localValue407 then State.aimLockTarget = nil; State.aimLockTargetPart = nil; return end
            local camera2 = workspace.CurrentCamera
            if not camera2 then return end
            local partOrPosition5 = camera2.CFrame
            local localValue211 = partOrPosition5.Position
            local localValue409 = partOrPosition5.LookVector
            local localValue212 = Config.aimLockSmoothness
            local distanceOrVector8 = localValue407 - localValue211
            local localValue410 = distanceOrVector8.Magnitude
            local localValue411 = (localValue407 - rootPart3.Position)["Magnitude"]
            local localValue412 = 4.0
            local distanceOrVector7
            if localValue410 >= localValue412 and localValue411 >= 1.5 then
                distanceOrVector7 = distanceOrVector8.Unit; State.aimLockTargetPart = distanceOrVector7
            elseif State.aimLockTargetPart then
                distanceOrVector7 = State.aimLockTargetPart
            else
                distanceOrVector7 = localValue409
            end
            local localValue191 = localValue409:Lerp(distanceOrVector7, localValue212)
            if localValue191.Magnitude < 0.001 then return end
            camera2.CFrame = CFrame.new(localValue211, localValue211 + localValue191)
            local localValue112 = character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            if localValue112 and not localValue112.AutoRotate then
                local localValue315 = camera2.CFrame.LookVector
                local localValue373 = rootPart3.Position
                local vector10 = Vector3.new(localValue315.X, 0, localValue315.Z)
                if vector10.Magnitude > 0.001 then
                    local localValue413 = math.atan2(rootPart3.CFrame.LookVector["X"], rootPart3.CFrame.LookVector["Z"])
                    local localValue414 = math.atan2(vector10.X, vector10.Z)
                    local localValue342 = localValue414 - localValue413
                    localValue342 = ((localValue342 + math.pi) % ((2.0) * math.pi)) - math.pi
                    local localValue415 = localValue413 + localValue342 * 0.15
                    local localValue416 = math.sin(localValue415)
                    local localValue417 = math.cos(localValue415)
                    local localValue418 = localValue373 + Vector3.new(localValue416, 0, localValue417) * (900.0)
                    rootPart3.CFrame = CFrame.new(localValue373, Vector3.new(localValue418.X, localValue373.Y, localValue418.Z))
                end
            end
        end)
    end

    function StopCameralock()
        if State.aimLockRenderConnection then State.aimLockRenderConnection.Disconnect(State.aimLockRenderConnection); State.aimLockRenderConnection = nil end
        State.aimLockTarget = nil; State.aimLockTargetPart = nil
    end
end

local function cameraRoutine15()
    cameraRoutine14()
    localValue420.m, localValue420.n = 1, 1
    pcall(function()
        RunService:UnbindFromRenderStep("NEKOPOI_Aspect")
        RunService:BindToRenderStep("NEKOPOI_Aspect", Enum.RenderPriority.Camera["Value"] + 2, function(localValue138)
            if not Config.stretchedResolutionEnabled then return end
            local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
            if not camera then return end
            local localValue422 = camera.ViewportSize
            if localValue422.Y < 1 then return end
            local localValue423 = localValue422.X / localValue422.Y
            local localValue424 = tonumber(Config.stretchedAspectRatio) or (16 / 9)
            local m, n = 1, 1
            if math.abs(localValue424 - localValue423) > 0.03 then
                if localValue424 < localValue423 then
                    m = 1
                    n = math.clamp(localValue424 / localValue423, 0.1, 1)
                else
                    m = math.clamp(localValue423 / localValue424, 0.1, 1)
                    n = (1.0)
                end
            end

            local localValue425 = math.clamp(localValue138 or 0.0167, 0.0005, 0.1)
            local localValue299 = math.clamp((1.0) - math.exp((-12.0) * localValue425), 0.001, 1)
            localValue420.m = localValue420.m + (m - localValue420.m) * localValue299
            localValue420.n = localValue420.n + (n - localValue420.n) * localValue299
            if math.abs(localValue420.m - 1) < 0.002 and math.abs(localValue420.n - (1.0)) < 0.002 then
                localValue420.m, localValue420.n = (1.0), 1
                return
            end
            local localValue301 = camera.CFrame
            local player = localValue301.Position
            if player.X ~= player.X or player.Y ~= player.Y or player.Z ~= player.Z then return end
            if localValue301.RightVector.Magnitude < 0.01 or localValue301.UpVector.Magnitude < 0.01 then return end
            local localValue426 = CFrame.fromMatrix(player, localValue301.RightVector.Unit, localValue301.UpVector.Unit)
            camera.CFrame = localValue426 * CFrame.new(0, 0, 0, localValue420.m, 0, (0.0), 0, localValue420.n, 0, (0.0), 0, 1)
        end)
    end)
end

local function cleanupRoutine4()

    if UserInputService.TouchEnabled and not UserInputService.KeyboardEnabled then
        return
    end

    State.localValue17 = UserInputService.MouseIconEnabled
    State.localValue18   = UserInputService.MouseBehavior

    UserInputService.MouseIconEnabled = true
    UserInputService.MouseBehavior     = Enum.MouseBehavior.Default

    if State.mouseIconConnection then State.mouseIconConnection.Disconnect(State.mouseIconConnection) end
    if State.mouseBehaviorConnection then State.mouseBehaviorConnection.Disconnect(State.mouseBehaviorConnection) end

    State.mouseIconConnection = UserInputService:GetPropertyChangedSignal("MouseIconEnabled"):Connect(function()
        if Config.forceMouseCursor and not UserInputService.MouseIconEnabled then
            UserInputService.MouseIconEnabled = true
        end
    end)

    State.mouseBehaviorConnection = UserInputService:GetPropertyChangedSignal("MouseBehavior"):Connect(function()
        if Config.forceMouseCursor and UserInputService.MouseBehavior == Enum.MouseBehavior.LockCenter then
            UserInputService.MouseBehavior = Enum.MouseBehavior.Default
        end
    end)
end

local function uiRoutine4()

        State.mouseIconConnection.Disconnect(State.mouseIconConnection)
        State.mouseIconConnection = nil
    end
    if State.mouseBehaviorConnection then
        State.mouseBehaviorConnection.Disconnect(State.mouseBehaviorConnection)
        State.mouseBehaviorConnection = nil
    end

    if State.localValue17 ~= nil then
        UserInputService.MouseIconEnabled = State.localValue17
        State.localValue17 = nil
    end
    if State.localValue18 ~= nil then
        UserInputService.MouseBehavior = State.localValue18
        State.localValue18 = nil
    end
end

    function _ApplyCopiedAvatarToUI()

local function setSkipEndScreenMode(darknessMode)
    darknessMode = darknessMode or false
    local camera = Workspace.CurrentCamera
    local localValue506 = State.skipEndScreenConns

    local localValue507 = false
    local localValue508 = false
    local localValue509 = false

    pcall(function()
        local child9 = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Remotes")
        if not child9 then return end

        local function remoteRoutine2(remote)
            if remote and remote:IsA("RemoteEvent") then
                for _, localValue124 in ipairs(getconnections(remote.OnClientEvent)) do
                    localValue124.Disable(localValue124)
                    localValue124.Disconnect(localValue124)
                end
            end
        end

        local child44 = child9:FindFirstChild("Game")
        if child44 then
            for _, name in ipairs({"cutscene", "cutsceneEnd", "cutsceneEnd2", "endscreencutscene", "cutsceneEndwithownchar", "shake"}) do
                remoteRoutine2(child44:FindFirstChild(name))
            end
        end

        local child39 = child9:FindFirstChild("Killers")
        if child39 then remoteRoutine2(child39:FindFirstChild("Startmori")) end

        if darknessMode then
            remoteRoutine2(child9:FindFirstChild("Darkness2"))
        end
    end)

    local function cameraRoutine16()
        if not camera then return end
        if camera.CameraType == Enum.CameraType.Scriptable then
            localValue507 = true
            camera.CameraType = Enum.CameraType.Custom
            localValue507 = false
        end
    end

    if camera then
        cameraRoutine16()
        table.insert(localValue506, camera:GetPropertyChangedSignal("CameraType"):Connect(function()
            if not localValue507 then cameraRoutine16() end
        end))

        table.insert(localValue506, camera:GetPropertyChangedSignal("FieldOfView"):Connect(function()
            if localValue508 or Config.lockFOVEnabled then return end
            if camera.FieldOfView ~= 70 then
                localValue508 = true
                camera.FieldOfView = (70.0)
                localValue508 = false
            end
        end))
    end

    table.insert(localValue506, Workspace:GetPropertyChangedSignal("CurrentCamera"):Connect(function()
        camera = Workspace.CurrentCamera
        if camera then cameraRoutine16() end
    end))

    table.insert(localValue506, LocalPlayer:GetAttributeChangedSignal("isspectating"):Connect(function()
        if localValue509 then return end
        if LocalPlayer:GetAttribute("isspectating") then
            localValue509 = true
            LocalPlayer:SetAttribute("isspectating", false)
            localValue509 = false
        end
    end))

    table.insert(localValue506, LocalPlayer:GetAttributeChangedSignal("killerend"):Connect(function()
        if localValue509 then return end
        if LocalPlayer:GetAttribute("killerend") then
            localValue509 = true
            LocalPlayer:SetAttribute("killerend", false)
            localValue509 = false
        end
    end))

    local function cleanupRoutine8()
        local localValue266 = Workspace:FindFirstChild("Map")
        if localValue266 then
            local child45 = localValue266:FindFirstChild("endscreen")
            if child45 then
                pcall(function() child45.Parent = nil end)
            end
        end

        local child46 = Workspace:FindFirstChild("BackgroundSounds")
        if child46 then
            pcall(function() child46.Destroy(child46) end)
        end
    end

    cleanupRoutine8()

    table.insert(localValue506, Workspace.DescendantAdded.Connect(Workspace.DescendantAdded,function(localValue123)
        if localValue123.Name == "endscreen" and localValue123.Parent and localValue123.Parent.Name == "Map" then
            task.wait(0.01)
            pcall(function() localValue123.Parent = nil end)
        elseif localValue123.Name == "BackgroundSounds" then
            pcall(function() localValue123.Destroy(localValue123) end)
        end
    end))

    local function uiRoutine18(gui)
        if not gui or not gui.Parent then return end
        pcall(function()

            if darknessMode and gui.Name == "Darkness" and gui:IsA("ScreenGui") then
                gui.Enabled = false
            end

            local function cleanupRoutine9(localValue123)
                if localValue123:IsA("VideoFrame") then
                    localValue123.Destroy(localValue123)
                elseif localValue123:IsA("Frame") and (localValue123.Name == "Frame2" or localValue123.Name == "blackout") then
                    localValue123.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                    if darknessMode then
                        localValue123.Visible = false

                        localValue123:GetPropertyChangedSignal("BackgroundTransparency"):Connect(function()
                            if localValue123.BackgroundTransparency < 1 then localValue123.BackgroundTransparency = 1 end
                        end)
                        localValue123:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Visible"):Connect(function()
                            if localValue123.Visible then localValue123.Visible = false end
                        end)
                    end
                elseif localValue123:IsA("ParticleEmitter") or localValue123:IsA("Beam") or localValue123:IsA("Trail") then
                    localValue123.Enabled = false
                end
                for _, localValue122 in ipairs(localValue123.GetChildren(localValue123)) do cleanupRoutine9(localValue122) end
            end
            cleanupRoutine9(gui)
        end)
    end

    for _, localValue122 in ipairs(PlayerGui.GetChildren(PlayerGui)) do
        local n = localValue122.Name
        if n == "Darkness" or n == "EndScreen" or n == "Cutscene" or n == "Results" then
            uiRoutine18(localValue122)
        end
    end

    table.insert(localValue506, PlayerGui.ChildAdded.Connect(PlayerGui.ChildAdded,function(localValue122)
        local n = localValue122.Name
        if n == "Darkness" or n == "EndScreen" or n == "Cutscene" or n == "Results" then
            task.wait(0.05)
            uiRoutine18(localValue122)
        end
    end))
end

local function resetEndScreenHooks()
    for _, localValue510 in ipairs(State.skipEndScreenConns) do

    end
    table.clear(State.skipEndScreenConns)
end

    local function featureRoutine12(localValue144, localValue145, speed, localValue146)

        local localValue150 = speed * math.sin(localValue145) * travelTime - (0.5 * localValue146 * travelTime * travelTime) - localValue149
        return localValue150, travelTime
    end
    local function spearRoutine8(localValue144, localValue151, localValue145, speed, localValue146)

    local function featureRoutine20()
        for _, player in ipairs(Players.GetPlayers(Players)) do
            if getPlayerRole(player) == "killer" then
                return player
            end
        end
        for _, player in ipairs(Players.GetPlayers(Players)) do
            local ok, team = pcall(function() return player.Team and player.Team.Name or "" end)
            if ok and tostring(team):lower():find("killer") then
                return player
            end
        end
        return nil
    end

    end

    registerTask("Flowstate", 0.1, function()

                    localValue755 = true
                    table.insert(localValue753, child57)
                end
            end
        end
        if localValue755 then triggerFlowstateCooldown() end

        for _, target in ipairs(localValue753) do
            if target then
                local cur = target:GetAttribute("Flowstate")
                if cur ~= active then
                    pcall(function() target:SetAttribute("Flowstate", active) end)
                end
            end
        end
    end)

local cleanupRoutine19, FP_SetPerk
do
    State.fpBuffs = State.fpBuffs or {}
    State.fpConns = State.fpConns or {}
    State.fpOn = State.fpOn or {}
    if State.fpCooldown == nil then State.fpCooldown = 10 end
    if State.fpLastEnd == nil then State.fpLastEnd = (0.0) end

    local function featureRoutine22()
        return LocalPlayer.Character
    end

    local function featureRoutine23()
        local localValue292 = 0
        local timestamp = tick()
        for _, localValue137 in pairs(State.fpBuffs or {}) do
            if timestamp < localValue137.endTime then localValue292 = localValue292 + localValue137.amt end
        end
        return localValue292
    end

    local function featureRoutine24()
        if getPlayerRole(LocalPlayer) == "killer" then return end
        local char = featureRoutine22()
        local localValue292 = featureRoutine23()
        if char then
            pcall(function()
                char:SetAttribute("speedboost", localValue292 > 0 and (1 + localValue292 / 14) or (1.0))
            end)
        end
    end

    function cleanupRoutine19(name, amt, dur)
        if getPlayerRole(LocalPlayer) == "killer" then return end
        if State.fpBuffs[name] then return end
        if tick() - State.fpLastEnd < (State.fpCooldown or 10) and next(State.fpBuffs) == nil then return end
        State.fpBuffs[name] = { amt = amt, endTime = tick() + dur }
        if name == "PerfectLanding" then State.fpPLAnnounced = false end
        featureRoutine24()
        notify("Fake Perks", "[" .. name .. "] Aktif! +" .. math.floor(amt / 14 * 100) .. "% Speed (" .. dur .. "s)", (3.0))
    end

    local function cleanupRoutine20(name)
        local localValue465 = State.fpConns[name]
        if localValue465 then
            for _, localValue301 in ipairs(localValue465) do pcall(function() localValue301.Disconnect(localValue301) end) end
            State.fpConns[name] = nil
        end
    end

    local function featureRoutine25(name, localValue124)
        if localValue124 == nil then return end
        if not State.fpConns[name] then State.fpConns[name] = {} end
        table.insert(State.fpConns[name], localValue124)
    end

    local function featureRoutine26(name, fn)
        featureRoutine25(name, LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded.Connect(LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded,fn))
        local cur = featureRoutine22()
        if cur then pcall(fn, cur) end
    end

    function FP_SetPerk(name, localValue392)
        if name == "Flowstate" then State.fpOn.Flowstate = nil; return end
        State.fpOn[name] = localValue392 and true or false
        if not localValue392 then
do local localValue757=(25*63+25)%56 end
            cleanupRoutine20(name)
            State.fpBuffs[name] = nil
            if name == "PerfectLanding" then State.fpPLAnnounced = false end
            featureRoutine24()
            return
        end
        if name == "QuickRecovery" then

            local function instanceRoutine30(_, sprintFlag)
                if not State.fpOn.QuickRecovery then return end
                if sprintFlag ~= true then return end
                cleanupRoutine19("QuickRecovery", 5.6, 3)
            end
            pcall(function()
                local child11 = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Remotes")
                local localValue275 = child11 and child11:FindFirstChild("Window")
                local instance24 = localValue275 and localValue275:FindFirstChild("Vaultbindable")
                if instance24 and instance24:IsA("BindableEvent") then featureRoutine25(name, instance24.Event.Connect(instance24.Event,instanceRoutine30)) end
            end)
        elseif name == "PerfectLanding" then
            featureRoutine26(name, function(ch)
                local localValue112 = ch:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                if not localValue112 then return end
                local localValue758, fallStart = false, 0
                featureRoutine25(name, localValue112.StateChanged.Connect(localValue112.StateChanged,function(_, new)
                    if not State.fpOn.PerfectLanding then return end
                    if new == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Freefall then
                        localValue758, fallStart = true, tick()
                    elseif localValue758 and (new == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Landed or new == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Running) then
                        localValue758 = false
                        if tick() - fallStart >= 0.25 then
                            cleanupRoutine19("PerfectLanding", 5.6, 3)
                        end
                    end
                end))
            end)
        elseif name == "AdrenalineRush" then
            featureRoutine26(name, function(ch)
                local localValue112 = ch:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                if not localValue112 then return end
                local localValue759 = localValue112.Health
                featureRoutine25(name, localValue112.HealthChanged.Connect(localValue112.HealthChanged,function(newHP)
                    if State.fpOn.AdrenalineRush and newHP < localValue759 and newHP <= 50 and newHP > (0.0) then
                        cleanupRoutine19("AdrenalineRush", 4, 5)
                    end
                    localValue759 = newHP
                end))
            end)
        end
    end

    function FP_ReArm()
        for name, localValue392 in pairs(State.fpOn or {}) do
            if localValue392 then
                pcall(FP_SetPerk, name, false)
                pcall(FP_SetPerk, name, true)
            end
        end
    end

    registerTask("FakePerks", 0.1, function()
        if next(State.fpBuffs) == nil then return end
        local timestamp = tick()
        local localValue760 = false
        for localValue761, localValue137 in pairs(State.fpBuffs) do
            if timestamp >= localValue137.endTime then
                State.fpBuffs[localValue761] = nil
                localValue760 = true
            end
        end
        if localValue760 and next(State.fpBuffs) == nil then
            State.fpLastEnd = timestamp
        end

        local localValue762 = State.fpBuffs.PerfectLanding
        if localValue762 and timestamp < localValue762.endTime then
            local char = LocalPlayer.Character
            if char then
                pcall(function()
                    if char:GetAttribute("MovementLocked") then
                        char:SetAttribute("MovementLocked", false)
                        if not State.fpPLAnnounced then
                            State.fpPLAnnounced = true
                            print("[FP] MovementLocked ditahan OFF (PerfectLanding)")
                        end
                    end
                end)
            end
        end
        featureRoutine24()
    end)
end

            local child11 = localValue266[localValue134] or (16 / 9)

                local timestamp2 = os.clock()

                    local char = LocalPlayer.Character
                    local localValue112 = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                    if localValue112 and localValue112.Health > 0 then
                        notify("Server", "Respawn berhasil", 2)
                        return
                    end
                    task.wait(0.5)
                end
                notify("Server", "Karakter mati - game menolak respawn paksa, tunggu round berikutnya", 3)
            end)
        end,
    })
